Proceeding ✅ — here is the Progressions Menu Integration Map (canon-ready, implementation-facing, no engine wiring).

Progressions Menu Integration Map v1.2
1) Where Progressions Live
Advanced Menu
Advanced → Musical Interaction → Progressions (primary home)

Create / edit / save / load progressions

Step list + Step Details + overrides

Links to Chord Pitch Set + optional Scale/Mode Pitch Set

No scroll, PWL overlay compliant

Basic Menu
No progression editing in Basic (canon-safe)

Basic may allow only:

Progression: Off / On

Select from Library (optional “teacher shortcut” later)

Rationale: prevents complexity drift and keeps Basic beginner-safe

2) Runtime Binding (What Progressions Affect)
When a progression is enabled and active, it determines:

2.1 “Current Harmonic Target”
A single authoritative object for the current prompt cycle:

currentStep

resolved chord target (from chord set + step constraints + overrides)

tonal context (progression active, possibly step-override)

2.2 What it does not control
Surface Controls roles (Prompt/Mark/Persistence/SAM)

Answer Set / Answer Count (handled by AnswerRules/Cardinality)

Timers/check clock (Time & Turn Flow section)

Progressions only set WHAT chord is active, not HOW the player interacts.

3) Interaction With Prompt Generation (Conceptual Binding Only)
Progression output feeds prompt generation as input constraints:

If gameplay domain is Chords:

Prompt material = the resolved chord from progression step

If gameplay domain is Arpeggios:

Prompt material may derive from:

progression step chord → arpeggio pitch set

If gameplay domain is Scales/Intervals:

Progression is generally ignored unless explicitly enabled as “harmonic context” later

This keeps the system deterministic and avoids hidden coupling.

4) Tonal Assist Placement and Write Targets
Location (locked)
Gameplay → Misc Information Panel → Tonal Assist module

What it can analyze
Active progression (all steps)

Active chord pitch set (builder/bank/hybrid)

Active scale/mode pitch set (if linked)

Recent play history window (bounded)

Add / Replace write targets (locked behavior)
When user selects a suggestion:

4.1 Replace
Replaces a single target (user chooses where it applies):

Replace Progression tonalContext.active

OR replace Step override tonalContext (if user is editing a step)

OR replace Linked Scale/Mode Pitch Set identity (if explicitly linked)

Rule: Replace must show a one-line summary of what changes.

4.2 Add
Adds without removing:

Adds to progression.tonalContext.library[]

Optionally adds a new Scale/Mode Pitch Set to the library (teacher workflow)

Adds tags/metadata for later reuse (library building goal)

No automatic propagation unless user chooses.

5) Progression Selection and Persistence
At match start
If a progression is selected and enabled:

bind it into the match options as progressionId + enabled=true

During gameplay
Step index persists until advancement rule triggers

Overrides are resolved per-step

If progression disabled mid-game:

progression stops influencing targets

current step index preserved for re-enable (optional; can be locked later)

6) Conflict Resolution Rules (Canon-Safe)
If conflicts exist:

Step override beats progression defaults

Progression defaults beat pitch set defaults

Chord Bank entries beat Builder constraints (Hybrid rule)

Chord Source = By Key locks diatonic enforcement ON

Surface Controls never override tonal eligibility

This preserves your “advanced escape hatch” without harming beginners.

7) Minimal UI Hooks (No new panels)
Advanced Menu
Progression editor + library actions

Gameplay
Misc Information Panel:

Tonal Assist (analysis + Add/Replace)

Optional “Current Progression Step” readout (non-interactive summary)

No floating windows required.

8) Optional (Later) Teacher Shortcut in Basic
If you want a fast classroom workflow later:

Basic: “Progression: Off/On”

Basic: “Progression Library Select”
No editing, only selection.

If you want the next safe artifact: I can generate the MatchOptions binding contract (exact fields that store selected Pitch Sets + selected Progression + Tonal Assist metadata) so implementation can wire without ambiguity.

1) Scale / Mode Pitch Set Schema v1.1
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "glg.pitchSet.scaleMode.schema.v1.1",
  "title": "GLG Scale/Mode Pitch Set",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["id", "type", "identity", "pattern", "constraints", "tonal"],
  "properties": {
    "id": { "type": "string" },
    "type": { "const": "scaleMode" },

    "identity": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["name", "rootPolicy"],
      "properties": {
        "name": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "Major",
            "Natural Minor",
            "Harmonic Minor",
            "Melodic Minor",
            "Ionian",
            "Dorian",
            "Phrygian",
            "Lydian",
            "Mixolydian",
            "Aeolian",
            "Locrian",
            "Minor Pentatonic",
            "Major Pentatonic",
            "Minor Blues",
            "Major Blues"
          ]
        },
        "rootPolicy": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": ["fixed", "relative", "byKey"]
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },

    "pattern": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["family"],
      "properties": {
        "family": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "notesPerString",
            "positionBox",
            "linear",
            "intervallic",
            "directional",
            "chromaticHybrid"
          ]
        },

        "notesPerString": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "min": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 4 },
            "max": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 4 }
          }
        },

        "positionBox": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "system": { "type": "string", "enum": ["CAGED", "BOX"] },
            "positions": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 5 }
            },
            "rotate": { "type": "boolean" }
          }
        },

        "intervallic": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "interval": { "type": "string" },
            "direction": { "type": "string", "enum": ["up", "down", "both"] }
          }
        },

        "directional": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "style": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": ["straight", "upDown", "zigZag", "pendulum", "custom"]
            }
          }
        },

        "chromaticHybrid": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "allowPassingTones": { "type": "boolean" },
            "explicitPitchClasses": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": { "type": "string" }
            }
          }
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },

    "constraints": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["strings", "span"],
      "properties": {
        "strings": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "allowed": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 6 }
            },
            "min": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 6 },
            "max": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 6 }
          }
        },
        "span": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "min": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 },
            "max": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0 }
          }
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    },

    "tonal": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["diatonicEnforced"],
      "properties": {
        "diatonicEnforced": { "type": "boolean" },
        "tonalAssist": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "enabled": { "type": "boolean" }
          }
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "additionalProperties": false
}
2) Chord Pitch Set Schema v1.1
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "glg.pitchSet.chord.schema.v1.1",
  "title": "GLG Chord Pitch Set",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["id", "type", "mode", "source", "constraints", "tonal"],
  "properties": {
    "id": { "type": "string" },
    "type": { "const": "chord" },

Guitar Learning Game — Canon Continuation (NO NEW DESIGN PHASE)

You are continuing an existing, long-running Guitar Learning Game project.
This is not a new design phase.

A) Canon Priority (Non-Negotiable)
Treat the following as authoritative sources of truth, in this order:
1) Guitar_Learning_Game_Authoritative_System_Manual_v1_0_EXHAUSTIVE.docx

Conflict rules:
- Documents override chat memory
- Later timestamps override earlier
- No assumptions allowed
- If unsure → ask before proceeding

B) Locked Definitions (Must Not Drift)
1) Answer Sets
- Single: one specific instance
- Equivalent: same staff note AND same octave mapped to all valid fretboard locations producing that exact pitch (octave differences NOT equivalent)
- Octave (future/current supported): same note name across a defined octave range
- All (future): all instances of the same note name

2) Visibility vs Persistence
- Boards are always visible
- “Persistence” = whether correct answers remain after marking
- Persistence configurable per surface
- “display/visible/visibility” are deprecated terms for this concept

3) Answer Set ≠ Answer Count
Default:
- Answer Set = Single
- Answer Count = 1

4) Surface Controls (Canon Roles)
Surface roles are exactly:
- Prompt
- Mark
- Persistence
- SAM (Show After Mark)
Each role supports Surface Scope: single | rotating | all | random
Coordination exists for Prompt/Mark/Persistence (multi-surface behavior), SAM does not require coordination.

5) Presets A–J (Locked Intent)
- A–G: Note Rail → Fretboard/Staff/TAB (various combinations)
- H–J: Board → Note Rail (recognition direction)
No renaming. Descriptions may be clarified but not reinterpreted.
For H/I/J: Note Rail should NOT persist by default; FB/ST/TAB should (persistence flipped vs earlier draft).

C) Current Phase / Where to Resume
Resume at:
Phase B-2 — Preset Definition Table + JSON Schema + Menu Completion

What is already locked:
- Pitch Sets (Scale/Mode, Chord, Arpeggio, Interval) conceptual design + Blues scales added
- Progressions system design supports mode-aware degrees, borrowed chords, secondary dominants via per-step overrides
- Tonal Assist:
  - Lives in Gameplay Misc Information Panel
  - Also callable from Advanced Menu builders as a guide that analyzes the CURRENT edited object
